NOTE: v53 transport rides syscall(173, 0xDEADBEEF, 0xFAFAFAFA, cmd, &payload) through the LIVE sys_call_table entry; the entry AND the whole accept path are clang-17; the uid gate reads the cred offset CALIBRATED at install from init_task/init_cred (no header-derived struct offsets); the SHOW cmd ids follow the STOCK CLI wire ABI (0x555e1=version 20B, 0x555e2=enabled_features 8196B, 0x555e3=variant 20B); v53 THE FEATURE RUNG forwards the three stage-3-armed feature cmds to the kernel handlers (0x60020=add_sus_map bit8, 0x55561=hide_sus_mnts bit1, 0x555b0=set_cmdline bit6 - ids and bits verified against BOTH the stock CLI and the base module switch at build time); retired-layer cmds (add_sus_path/add_sus_path_loop/set_uname/kstat) stay not-handled (CLI keeps 126), the v49 GCC wrapper is never called.
